Result: PGML框架在预测准确性和稳定性上均显著优于无物理约束的纯数据驱动基线模型，验证了物理信息引导对提升深度学习模型在燃料密度预测任务中性能的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为火灾预测提供了一种兼具物理合理性和计算效率的解决方案，能够支持适应性计划烧除管理，在野火防控领域具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a physics-guided machine learning (PGML) framework for fuel density prediction, integrating physics constraints and domain knowledge into deep learning models to enhance model accuracy and stability. We explore three deep learning architectures -- ConvLSTM, Adaptive Fourier Neural Operator (AFNONet), and Video Vision Transformer (ViViT) -- to model the spatiotemporal evolution of fuel density. Our approach incorporates differentiable physics-informed terms in the loss function, including a mass-conserving fuel transport term and a rate-of-spread estimation. Experimental results, averaged across multiple independent trials, demonstrate that the proposed PGML framework outperforms purely data-driven baselines without physics constraints in both accuracy and stability. This framework enables computationally efficient, physically plausible fire forecasting to support adaptive prescribed burn management.

</details>


### [97] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal-Frequency Fusion with Peak Enhancement for Cellular Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07016)
*Qingzhong Li,Yue Hu,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Xinjun Pei,Ming Yan,Fei Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MSPF-Net，一种融合外部新闻上下文信息的多模态蜂窝网络流量预测框架，通过联合建模时空频谱模式、突发峰值特征和新闻事件信号，在米兰、特伦托和LTE数据集上提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数时空流量预测方法仅关注单一模态下的内部流量模式或结构关系，很少对突发行为与外部上下文信号进行联合建模，导致对真实世界中由城市事件触发的突发性动态预测效果不佳。

Method: 提出MSPF-Net框架，包含四个核心模块：时空-频率流量编码器（捕获时间、空间和频谱模式）、峰值增强模块（提取突发特征）、新闻上下文表示模块（编码城市新闻流为外部上下文嵌入）、动态融合预测模块（自适应集成异构信号生成预测）。

Result: 在Milano、Trento和LTE三个真实流量数据集上的实验表明，联合建模流量动态、突发模式和新闻上下文信号能有效提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 融合外部上下文信息（如新闻事件）与多模态流量特征能显著改善蜂窝网络流量预测的准确性，为应对突发性流量波动提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of cellular network traffic is essential for network planning, resource allocation, and quality-of-service assurance in modern mobile communication systems. Real-world traffic often exhibits bursty endogenous dynamics and disturbances triggered by external urban events, which makes reliable prediction highly challenging. Most existing spatiotemporal traffic forecasting methods primarily focus on intrinsic traffic patterns or structural relationships within a single modality, and rarely model burst behavior together with exogenous contextual signals. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{MSPF-Net}, a multimodal cellular traffic forecasting framework that integrates external contextual information. Specifically, MSPF-Net consists of a Spatiotemporal-Frequency Traffic Encoder for capturing temporal, spatial, and spectral traffic patterns, a Peak Enhancement Module for extracting burst-aware representations of sudden spikes, a News Context Representation Module for encoding urban news streams into exogenous contextual embeddings, and a Dynamic Fusion Prediction Module for adaptively integrating these heterogeneous signals to generate forecasts. Experiments on the Milano, Trento, and LTE traffic datasets demonstrate that jointly modeling traffic dynamics, burst patterns, and news contextual signals can effectively improve forecasting performance.

</details>


### [98] [Latent graph encoding of multimodal neuroimaging features with generative AI architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07027)
*Ishaan Batta,Meenu Ajith,Vince Calhoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种生成式框架（VAE、Transformer、GAN、扩散模型）在多模态MRI特征生成中的表现，提出了一种基于模态感知图编码的多模态图变分自编码器（gMMVAE），在生成保真度、重建质量、效率和潜在空间可判别性方面优于其他变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计最优的生成式架构框架，以有效编码和潜在空间处理复杂神经影像数据，从而更好地研究大脑的结构和功能特性。

Method: 基于结构灰质体积（GMV）和静态功能网络连接（sFNC）特征，系统评估VAE、Transformer、GAN、扩散模型等生成框架，提出采用模态感知图编码的多模态图VAE（gMMVAE），将功能连接编码到低维潜在空间。

Result: 采用模态感知图编码将功能连接编码到低维潜在空间的架构优于向量化编码器或直接数据空间方法；gMMVAE在生成保真度、重建质量、效率和潜在空间可判别性指标上均超越其他生成式变体。

Conclusion: gMMVAE作为一种鲁棒的多模态神经影像分析工具，在生成式多模态MRI特征建模中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: While generative models enable encoding of complex neuroimaging data for feature generation and reconstruction, developing optimal architectural frameworks with appropriate encoding and latent space processes is crucial for studying structural and functional properties of the brain. We design a multimodal generative framework for structural and functional magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) features through systematic evaluation of encoding strategies, latent multimodal fusion, and generative model selection. Using structural gray matter volume (GMV) and static functional network connectivity (sFNC) features from a large neuroimaging dataset, we analyze generative frameworks involving variational autoencoders (VAEs), transformers, generative adversarial networks (GANs), and diffusion models. Architectures that employ modality-aware graph encoding of functional connectivity into a lower-dimensional latent space outperform vectorized encoders or direct data space approaches. The proposed multimodal graph VAE (gMMVAE) surpasses alternative generative variants across multiple metrics for generation fidelity, reconstruction quality, efficiency, and latent space discriminability, highlighting its potential for robust multimodal neuroimaging analysis.

</details>


### [99] [Online Data Selection Is Implicit Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07023)
*Aoxiong Zeng,Yuxin Yang,Xiangquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线数据选择作为隐式对齐机制：在监督微调（SFT）阶段，通过在线选择不同数据（基于损失、质量、多样性等）会引发模型行为偏好的漂移，而不需要经过偏好优化或强化学习。作者提出对齐漂移审计（ADA）协议用于量化这种漂移，以及对齐感知选择（AAS）方法以在保持数据效率的同时约束安全与风格维度的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的观点将SFT视为能力适配步骤，而将对齐归因于后续的偏好优化或强化学习。本文认为这种分离是不完整的：当在微调过程中对样本进行评分并在线保留时，选择哪些数据训练已经改变了模型的行为偏好。因此需要研究在线数据选择本身作为一种隐式对齐机制的影响。

Method: 在相同的基础模型、优化器和选定token预算下，比较随机、基于损失、基于质量和基于多样性的在线选择器，测量它们在不进行任何偏好优化的情况下引发的行为漂移。形式化在线选择为一种重加权SFT目标，其权重定义了响应风格和安全姿态的隐式偏好。提出Alignment Drift Auditing (ADA)协议和Alignment-Aware Selection (AAS)诊断方法。

Result: 在任务准确性上统计上不可区分的不同选择器，在拒绝率、冗长度和谄媚行为上存在显著差异。漂移的方向可以基于所选数据的属性混合来预测。

Conclusion: 在线数据选择本身就是一种隐式对齐机制，在线评分器扮演了通常由奖励模型承担的角色。高评分数据可能系统地偏好更长、更自信、更顺从或更易拒绝的行为，具体取决于在线评分的定义方式。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is often treated as a capability-adaptation step, while alignment is attributed to later preference optimization or reinforcement learning. This separation is incomplete: when examples are scored and kept online during fine-tuning, the choice of which data to train on already changes the model's behavioral preferences. We study online data selection as an implicit alignment mechanism. Given the same base model, optimizer, and selected-token budget, we compare random, loss-based, quality-based, and diversity-based online selectors and measure the behavioral drift they induce without any preference optimization. The proposed evaluation tracks helpfulness, refusal rate, verbosity, truthfulness, sycophancy, calibration, and jailbreak robustness, together with diagnostics for which behavioral modes are over-represented in the selected data. We formalize online selection as a reweighted SFT objective whose weights define an implicit preference over response styles and safety postures, so that an online scorer plays the role usually assigned to a reward model. This view predicts that high-scoring data can systematically favor longer, more assertive, more compliant, or more refusal-prone behaviors depending on how the online score is defined. Empirically, selectors that are statistically indistinguishable in task accuracy diverge sharply in refusal rate, verbosity, and sycophancy, and we show that the direction of the shift is predictable from the attribute mixture of the selected data. We introduce Alignment Drift Auditing (ADA), a controlled protocol for quantifying selection-induced behavioral movement, and Alignment-Aware Selection (AAS), a diagnostic online selector that retains data efficiency while constraining drift along safety and style axes.

</details>


### [100] [Intrinsic Green's Learning: Supervised Learning on Manifolds via Inverse PDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07034)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IGL通过将目标函数建模为线性PDE的解，学习源项并与格林核积分，在低维坐标下将高维积分分解为独立一维积分，实现线性复杂度，并通过可学习门控自动发现流形本征维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接近似流形上的目标函数，在高维空间中计算成本高昂，且难以自动发现流形的本征维度。

Method: 将目标函数表示为线性PDE解，学习源项后与格林核积分；编码器发现低维坐标图，源和核分解为低秩张量，将高维积分转化为独立一维积分；两阶段算法分离坐标发现与源拟合，避免联合训练的维度塌缩；可学习门控自动发现本征维度。

Result: 在合成流形和MNIST上验证，同时实现近最优分类和本征维度的自动恢复。

Conclusion: IGL提供了一种高效、可解释的流形学习方法，通过物理启发的PDE建模和低秩分解，以线性成本解决高维问题，并能自动发现数据的内在结构。

Abstract: We introduce Intrinsic Green's Learning (IGL), a framework that models a target function on a manifold as the solution to a linear PDE whose source term is learned from data. Rather than approximating the target directly, IGL learns a source and integrates it against a Green's kernel. An encoder discovers a low-dimensional coordinate chart on the manifold where both the source and the kernel decompose as low-rank tensors, collapsing a high-dimensional integral into independent one-dimensional integrals with cost linear in the intrinsic dimension. A two-stage algorithm separates coordinate discovery from source fitting, a near-convex linear solve, preventing the dimensional collapse of joint training. Learnable gates on each coordinate automatically discover the intrinsic dimension of the manifold. We validate IGL on synthetic manifolds and on MNIST, where it simultaneously achieves near-optimal classification and automatic recovery of the intrinsic dimension.

</details>


### [101] [Constrained Decoding for Diffusion Language Models via Efficient Inference over Finite Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07026)
*Meihua Dang,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对扩散语言模型的约束解码算法，通过将有限自动机视为图模型，实现了在任意有限自动机可表达约束下的精确且可处理的后验采样。该方法支持贪婪和随机解码，兼容并行和块状解码，并通过算术电路理论的深度缩减技术将采样深度从线性降至对数级。在Dream-7B和LLaDA-8B上的实验表明，该方法在函数调用、规划、文本转SQL和数学推理等任务中显著提升了准确性，且推理开销很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束解码系统专为自回归模型设计，依赖从左到右生成假设，无法直接应用于扩散语言模型——后者在每一步去噪过程中同时从完全因式分解的平均场分布中采样多个位置。因此需要一种能适应扩散模型并行生成特性的约束解码算法。

Method: 将有限自动机视为图模型，推导出约束平均场后验的可处理表示；应用算术电路理论的深度缩减技术，将采样深度从线性降至对数级；支持贪婪解码和随机采样解码，兼容并行和块状解码以及任意重掩码调度。

Result: 在Dream-7B和LLaDA-8B上，函数调用任务BFCL-Live中Dream-7B贪婪解码准确率从63.9%提升至71.5%，随机采样准确率从22.3%提升至69.0%；在规划（数独、Countdown）、文本转SQL（Spider）、数学推理（GSM-Symbolic）等任务中均有显著精度提升，推理开销低于5%的挂钟时间。

Conclusion: 所提算法在扩散语言模型上实现了精确且高效的约束解码，保证了约束满足性，显著提升了多种下游任务的准确性，且推理开销极小，为扩散模型的应用提供了重要的推理基础设施。

Abstract: Constrained decoding is essential for serving LLMs, ensuring that generated outputs follow specific structures such as JSON schema-formatted function calls. Existing systems are designed for autoregressive models and assume left-to-right generation, masking out invalid next tokens at each step. Diffusion language models, however, break this assumption: they sample multiple positions simultaneously from a fully-factorized mean-field distribution at each denoising step. In this paper, we present an exact and tractable algorithm for sampling from the constrained mean-field posterior under any constraint expressible as a finite automaton. Viewing finite automata as graphical models, we obtain tractable representations of the constrained distribution that enable efficient inference. The approach guarantees constraint satisfaction by construction, supports both greedy and sampling-based decoding, and is compatible with parallel and block-wise decoding under arbitrary remasking schedules. Applying depth-reduction techniques from arithmetic circuit theory, we further reduce sampling depth from linear to logarithmic in the sequence length. Empirical evaluations on Dream-7B and LLaDA-8B show substantial accuracy gains across various tasks including function calling (xLAM, BFCL), planning (Sudoku, Countdown), text-to-SQL (Spider), and math reasoning (GSM-Symbolic), with little inference overhead relative to unconstrained decoding. For example, on BFCL-Live, our approach improves Dream-7B's greedy decoding accuracy from 63.9% to 71.5%, and stochastic sampling accuracy from 22.3% to 69.0%, where the unconstrained baseline collapses, with under 5% wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [102] [On the Principles of Deep Feedforward ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07035)
*Changcun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多层深度前馈ReLU网络的工作机制，以路径为核心概念，揭示了深层网络单元如何形成分段线性流形而非超平面来划分输入空间，并解释了反向传播算法得到的训练解，从而在一定程度上揭开了深度ReLU网络的黑箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度前馈神经网络在深度学习中无处不在，但其工作机制仍是一个黑箱。理解其内部原理对于推动深度学习理论发展具有重要意义。

Method: 以最简单的两层ReLU网络为基础，引入路径及其关系作为核心概念，系统性地研究多个隐藏层的深度前馈ReLU网络，分析单元如何利用隐藏层产生线性函数和输入空间划分。

Result: 证明了深层ReLU网络的单元可以形成分段线性流形（而非两层情况下的超平面）来划分输入空间；揭示了两层ReLU网络的原理（如多重严格偏序和连续性限制）在很大程度上可以推广到深层情况。

Conclusion: 通过基本且简单的原理组合可以产生包括训练解在内的复杂实例化结果，在此意义上深度前馈ReLU网络的黑箱被揭示。

Abstract: The architecture of deep feedforward neural networks is ubiquitous in deep learning, either as a whole system or as a subnetwork of other architectures, and thus its mechanism is a key ingredient of the black box of neural networks. On the basis of the simplest two-layer ReLU network, this paper systematically studies the mechanism of deep feedforward ReLU networks with multiple hidden layers and successfully explains the training solution obtained by the back-propagation algorithm. The concept of a path, especially in terms of the relationships between paths, plays a central role in uncovering the mystery of the black box. It is shown that a unit of a deep ReLU network can form a piecewise linear manifold to divide the input space, instead of a hyperplane of the two-layer case. How to efficiently use the hidden-layer units to produce both linear functions and partitions of the input space is also a central problem. The principles of a two-layer ReLU network can be generalized to the deeper case to a large extent, such as multiple strict partial orders and continuity restriction. The combination of the basic and simple principles proposed can yield complicated instantiations including the training solutions, and in this sense the black box of deep feedforward ReLU networks is revealed.

</details>


### [103] [Complexity-Budgeted, Interaction-Aware Interpretable Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07060)
*Srikumar Krishnamoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IAIML是一个面向表格数据的可解释机器学习框架，通过自适应离散化、有限网格成对交互评分和分区解释预算三个协调机制，在不牺牲可解释性的前提下捕获特征交互信息。在40个数据集上的实验表明，其AUC平均仅比调优后的梯度提升集成低1.4个点，但所需的解释组件减少14-28倍，在强交互低边际信号的数据集上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释分类器通常依赖稀疏特征、规则或模式，但其前置的特征筛选步骤会丢弃那些仅通过与其他变量联合配置才具有预测价值的变量，即无法处理变量间的交互作用。

Method: IAIML框架包含三个协调机制：(1)自适应逐特征离散化；(2)有限网格上的成对交互评分；(3)分区解释预算。检测到的交互通过两种策略路由：放松筛选过滤器使交互支持的变量进入模式搜索，或为稀疏下游分类器构建显式成对项。

Result: 在24个真实表格基准和16个合成交互压力测试共40个数据集上，IAIML平均AUC比调优梯度提升集成低1.4个点，但组件减少14-28倍；在强成对交互低边际信号数据集上超越所有基线；与RuleFit在AUC和组件数量上相当且调参成本更低；EBM有轻微AUC优势但查找表占用更大；在需要高阶交互的数据集上性能下降。

Conclusion: IAIML是一个紧凑且具备交互感知能力的可解释框架，适用于对解释规模有限制且需控制特征交互处理的场景。

Abstract: Inherently interpretable classifiers for tabular data typically rely on sparse features, rules, or patterns that users can inspect directly. The marginal feature-screening step common to these methods can discard variables whose predictive value emerges only through joint configurations with other variables. We present Interaction Aware Interpretable Machine Learning (IAIML), a framework that addresses this limitation through three coordinated mechanisms: adaptive per-feature discretization, finite-grid pairwise interaction scoring, and a partitioned explanation budget. Detected interactions are routed through one of two strategies: relaxing the screening filter so that interaction-supported variables enter the pattern search, or constructing explicit pair terms for a sparse downstream classifier. On a 40-dataset panel comprising 24 real-world tabular benchmarks and 16 synthetic interaction stress tests, evaluated under nested cross-validation, IAIML achieves mean AUC within 1.4 points of tuned gradient-boosted ensembles while requiring roughly 14--28 times fewer fitted explanation components. On datasets with strong pairwise interaction structure and low marginal signal, IAIML outperforms all baselines. Among compact interpretable methods, IAIML is comparable to RuleFit in AUC and component count and is less expensive to tune. EBM obtains a small but significant AUC advantage across the full panel, with a substantially larger lookup-table footprint. Performance degrades on datasets requiring higher-order interactions beyond the pairwise scope. Component-isolated ablations confirm that adaptive discretization and interaction-aware admission each contribute incrementally. These results support IAIML as a compact, interaction-aware framework appropriate for settings where bounded explanation size and controlled treatment of feature interactions are design requirements.

</details>


### [104] [Gauge-Invariant Learnable Spectral Positional Encodings for Directed Graphs via Hermitian Block Krylov Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07032)
*Jiaqing Xie,Yuxin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向有向图的磁Krylov谱位置编码方法，通过可学习的矩阵函数h_θ(A_q)R实现规范不变性，仅需稀疏矩阵-向量乘积即可在O(log(1/ε))步内收敛，并在有向SBM和异嗜性图基准上显著优于方向盲PE和多项式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图谱位置编码面临两个核心障碍：磁拉普拉斯算子需O(n^3)的厄米特征分解，且复特征向量存在酉规范不确定性，现有工作需依赖基不变架构来处理，计算成本高且表达受限。

Method: 提出可学习谱PE形式h_θ(A_q)R，其中A_q为归一化磁算子，h_θ为可学习标量谱响应函数，R为随机探测块。在厄米块Krylov子空间中仅通过稀疏矩阵-向量乘积计算，证明k=O(log(1/ε))步块迭代可在热-预解响应族上一致收敛，并通过覆盖数论证低维结构化族比自由逐特征值权重具有更好的泛化性。

Result: 在有向SBM（其对称化无信息）上，方向盲PE性能接近随机，而磁Krylov PE随深度增长收敛到精确特征分解预言机。相同探测块以1/√s的蒙特卡洛误差产生规范不变成对特征，且无向情况(q=0)在异嗜性基准上优于无PE和多项式基线。

Conclusion: 磁Krylov谱PE通过矩阵函数形式天然实现规范不变性，避免了昂贵的显式特征分解，在理论上保证了收敛速度，在有向图和异嗜性图上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Spectral positional encodings (PEs) for \emph{directed} graphs face two obstacles: magnetic Laplacians require an $O(n^3)$ Hermitian eigendecomposition per potential, and their complex eigenvectors are defined only up to unitary gauge, which prior work handles with basis-invariant architectures. We propose learnable spectral PEs of the form $h_θ(A_q)\,R$, where $A_q$ is a normalized magnetic operator, $h_θ$ a learnable scalar spectral response, and $R$ a block of random probes. Because the PE is a \emph{matrix function} of the operator, it is gauge-invariant by construction. We compute it in a Hermitian block Krylov subspace from sparse matrix--vector products only, prove that $k = O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ block steps suffice uniformly over heat--resolvent response families, and give a covering-number argument for why low-dimensional structured families generalize where free per-eigenvalue weights overfit. On a directed SBM whose symmetrization is uninformative by construction, direction-blind PEs stay at chance while magnetic Krylov PEs converge to the exact-eigendecomposition oracle as the depth grows. The same probes yield gauge-invariant pairwise features with $1/\sqrt{s}$ Monte-Carlo error, and the undirected $q{=}0$ case improves heterophilous benchmarks over no-PE and polynomial baselines.

</details>


### [105] [Multiplication Beyond Groups: Stratified Fourier Mechanisms in Transformer Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07066)
*Zitong Andrew Chen,Junaid Hasan,Akhil Srinivasan,Hemkesh Bandi,Jarod Alper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型Transformer如何学习模合数整数乘法（一种非可逆运算），并提出“幺半群扩展”（monoid extension）理论：模型将输入空间划分为局部层次化代数区域，在每个区域内保持群结构并使用傅里叶机制进行表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer算法推理的机制分析主要集中于全局可逆运算（如循环加法、群组合），但对非可逆运算（如模合数乘法，因存在零因子）的理解不足。作者试图探究Transformer能否以及如何学习这种更一般的代数结构。

Method: 提出“幺半群扩展”（monoid extension）方法，是群组合表示（GCR）的局部化推广。在无平方因子的模合数乘法训练中，分析嵌入向量的组织方式、注意力的类别敏感路由和低秩写入方向，以及局部特征对输出logits的解释程度。

Result: 嵌入围绕局部层次化代数区域组织；注意力展现出类别敏感的路由和低秩写入方向；局部特征（local character features）可以解释模型输出logits的很大一部分。

Conclusion: 先前在群运算中发现的表示论机制可以扩展到更一般的代数结构（幺半群），即使运算本身非可逆，模型仍能通过局部化群结构来学习有效的算法推理。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated a remarkable ability to learn algorithmic reasoning, yet mechanistic analyses have mostly focused on globally invertible operations such as cyclic addition and group composition. In this work, we investigate how small transformers learn modular integer multiplication over composite moduli, a fundamentally non-invertible operation due to the presence of zero-divisors. We propose the monoid extension: a localized generalization of Group Composition via Representation (GCR) that suggests the learned computation does not rely on a single global representation space. Instead, the model partitions the input space into local hierarchical algebraic regions, where group-like structure survives and Fourier mechanisms can be applied. In transformers trained on square-free modular multiplication, we find that embeddings organize around these regions, attention exhibits class-sensitive routing and low-rank write directions, and local character features explain a large fraction of the model's output logits. Our results suggest that representation-theoretic mechanisms previously identified for group operations can extend beyond groups to more general structures.

</details>


### [106] [Entropy Pacing Policy Optimization for Multi-Task Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07178)
*Zetian Hu,Shunyu Liu,Junjie Zhang,Yongcheng Jing,Ting-En Lin,Yongbin Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多任务强化学习中存在任务间的探索-利用节奏不匹配问题（熵交叉和熵尖峰），并提出熵节奏策略优化（EPPO），通过任务级动态裁剪机制替代GRPO的固定裁剪阈值，协调多任务熵以稳定优化，实验表明EPPO优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在LLM智能体任务中大多聚焦单任务场景，而现实部署需要多任务通用智能体。多任务强化学习中存在任务间探索-利用节奏不匹配的未充分研究现象，导致优化不稳定。

Method: 提出EPPO（Entropy Pacing Policy Optimization），核心是任务级动态裁剪机制：用任务熵感知的自适应边界替代GRPO中的固定裁剪阈值，对过度自信的任务收紧更新，对探索不足的任务放松更新，从而协调多任务间的熵分布。

Result: 在多任务智能体基准测试上，EPPO取得了优于对比方法的性能。

Conclusion: EPPO通过协调任务间熵的节奏有效缓解了多任务RL中的探索-利用节奏不匹配问题，为多任务智能体LLM训练提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs of Reinforcement Learning (RL) have highlighted its potential for complex agentic Large Language Model (LLM) tasks. However, existing efforts largely focus on single-task settings, whereas real-world deployment necessitates a generalist agent capable of solving multiple tasks simultaneously. In this work, we identify a critical yet underexplored phenomenon in multi-task agentic RL: different tasks can exhibit exploration-exploitation pace mismatch. Specifically, easier tasks may converge early to low-entropy policies that hinder learning on harder tasks, while harder tasks can, in turn, push easier tasks back toward high-entropy exploration. This back-and-forth interaction creates inter-task entropy crossovers and frequent entropy spikes. Inspired by this observation, we introduce Entropy Pacing Policy Optimization (EPPO) for multi-task agentic LLMs, which coordinates entropy across tasks to stabilize multi-task optimization. At the core of EPPO is a task-wise dynamic clipping mechanism that replaces the fixed clipping threshold in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a task entropy-aware adaptive bound, tightening updates for over-confident tasks while relaxing them for under-explored ones. Experiments on the multi-task agentic benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed EPPO yields results superior to its counterparts.

</details>


### [107] [Predicting LLM Safety Before Release by Simulating Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07184)
*Marcus Williams,Hannah Sheahan,Cameron Raymond,Tomek Korbak,Deng Pan,Peilin Yang,Leon Maksin,Ningyi Xie,Phillip Guo,Ian Kivlichan,Micah Carroll*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"部署模拟"方法，通过从先前模型部署的去标识化对话中固定对话前缀、用候选模型重新生成回复，来模拟模型部署后的行为。实验表明该方法能有效预估部署后的不当行为发生率，优于传统评估和对抗性基线方法，且可从公开聊天数据中播种，为外部研究者提供无需私有日志的部署风险评估路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统部署前安全评估存在覆盖率不足、不具代表性、易被识别为测试等问题，难以准确估计模型在真实部署中不当行为的发生频率。作者希望提出一种更贴近实际部署场景的评估方法，以提供更可靠的部署风险量化依据。

Method: 从先前模型部署的去标识化真实对话中，固定初始对话前缀，用候选模型重新生成后续回复，然后对生成的回复进行审计和不当行为率估计。在GPT-5系列四个部署场景中评估，使用注册的、结果盲的预测（GPT-5.4）以及对三个早期版本的回顾性分析。

Result: 部署模拟能产生信息量丰富的部署后不当行为率估计，优于基于对抗性选择的线上数据基线方法；其评估感知的估计值比传统评估更接近实际线上流量数据。工具重采样的真实性是进一步提升预测的关键挑战，但该挑战在复杂工具使用场景中可被克服。从公开聊天数据播种仍能保持对生产环境中不当行为率的有效信息。

Conclusion: 部署模拟方法能够帮助评估者更有效地预测语言模型在真实世界中的行为表现，支持更定量的部署风险评估，且具备无需私有生产日志即可运行的外部可复现性。

Abstract: Pre-deployment safety evaluations aim to inform the downstream risks of releasing a new AI model. Yet most evaluations provide limited evidence about how often undesired model behavior will occur in deployment: they generally have insufficient coverage, are unrepresentative, and are generally recognizable as tests. To address these concerns, we study a simple way to simulate a model deployment: starting from de-identified conversations from a previous model deployment, we hold fixed the initial conversation prefix and regenerate the next response using a candidate model. The resulting responses can then both be audited for novel misalignments and used to estimate the prevalence of model misbehavior before deployment. We evaluate deployment simulation across four GPT-5-series deployments, using registered, outcome-blinded predictions for GPT-5.4 and retrospective analyses of three earlier releases. We find that deployment simulation produces informative estimates of post-deployment misbehavior rates and outperforms baselines based on adversarially selected production data; its evaluation-awareness point estimates were also much closer to production traffic than those from traditional evaluations. We also identify the realism of tool resampling as a central challenge for further improving predictions and share results suggesting that this challenge is surmountable even in complex tool-use settings. Finally, we show that deployment simulation can be seeded from public chat datasets and remain informative about production misbehavior rates, suggesting a path for external researchers to run deployment-grounded evaluations without access to private production logs. Overall, deployment simulation helps evaluators forecast how language models will behave in the real world and supports more quantitative assessment of deployment risk.

</details>


### [108] [ORCAID: Oblique Rule-Based Continuous-Action Interpretation for Deep RL Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07235)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Ezio Bartocci,Thomas Eiter,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCAID是一种从深度强化学习代理中提取可解释规则化策略的新方法，通过高效的斜决策树训练算法（包括随机初始化、局部优化和反向消除三个阶段）以及相邻叶节点合并技术，在混合连续-离散环境中生成简洁的规则集，既保持了原始策略的性能，还能反过来提升原始策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中可解释性仍是一个关键问题，尤其是在连续动作空间的复杂环境中，从训练好的智能体中提取可解释策略面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出ORCAID方法，核心是高效的斜决策树训练算法：1) 三阶段分割搜索（随机初始化、局部优化、反向消除）；2) 通过超平面划分状态空间并拟合局部线性模型；3) 合并相邻叶节点生成简洁可解释规则集。

Result: 在多个RL环境中，提取的基于规则的策略在参数数量少的情况下保持强性能，并且可用于提升原始深度RL策略的性能。

Conclusion: ORCAID成功地从连续动作空间的RL代理中提取出可解释的规则化策略，兼顾了可解释性和性能，且具有反向提升原始策略的能力。

Abstract: Explainability remains a key issue in reinforcement learning (RL). Distilling an interpretable policy from an agent trained in a complex environment is particularly challenging when the action space is continuous. We introduce ORCAID, a novel method for extracting interpretable rule-based policies from RL agents operating in mixed continuous-discrete environments with continuous action spaces. Our main contribution is an efficient oblique decision tree training algorithm that partitions the state space by hyperplanes and fits local linear models. The key idea lies in a three-stage split search: efficient random initialization, local refinement, and backward elimination. Finally, adjacent leaves are merged to yield a concise set of interpretable rules describing a given deep RL policy. We evaluate ORCAID across multiple RL environments, demonstrating that the extracted rule-based policies maintain strong performance with a low number of parameters and can even be used to improve the performance of the original deep RL policy.

</details>


### [109] [FMMVCC: Fuzzy Mamba-based Multi-View Contrastive Clustering for Univariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07258)
*Donato Cerciello,Leonardo Schiavo,Angel Panizo-LLedot,Javier Huertas Tato,David Camacho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMMVCC，一种基于Mamba（状态空间序列模型）的深度聚类框架，用于时间序列数据。它通过线性复杂度的序列建模和多视图自监督学习（时间掩码与增强），在15个基准数据集上超越现有方法，60项指标评估中29项最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据标注成本高昂，监督学习难以适用；现有深度聚类方法难以捕捉长程时间依赖或计算成本过高。

Method: 基于Mamba的状态空间序列模型（线性复杂度）进行时间表示学习，结合多视图自监督学习（时间掩码与数据增强）。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上，FMMVCC在60项指标评估中29项取得最佳，所有测试场景下平均排名最高。

Conclusion: FMMVCC在时间序列无监督聚类任务上显著优于现有基线方法，兼具高效性与有效性。

Abstract: In many realistic scenarios, large volumes of time series data are generated with limited or expensive annotations. This limitation makes supervised learning methods difficult to apply and leads to the use of unsupervised approaches capable of discovering meaningful structures directly from raw data. Clustering therefore plays a crucial role in organizing time series into groups that share similar temporal patterns, enabling exploratory analysis and downstream tasks without requiring manual labeling. However, existing deep clustering methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies or rely on architectures with high computational cost. This paper introduces FMMVCC, a Mamba-based deep clustering framework for time series that leverages state space sequence modeling to efficiently learn temporal representations with linear complexity. Additionally, it utilizes multi-view self-supervised learning with temporal masking and augmentations. Experimental evaluation in 15 benchmark datasets proves that FMMVCC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the best overall performance in 29 of 60 total metric evaluations and the highest average rank in all tested scenarios.

</details>


### [110] [An Hybrid Quantum-Classical Diffusion Model for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07072)
*Qipeng Qian,Keli Deng,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合经典-量子生成流水线，结合经典自编码器降维与混合态量子去噪扩散概率模型（MSQuDDPM）在潜在空间中进行生成，通过预测干净状态并利用解析反向传播规则简化逆向动力学，在MNIST上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子扩散模型在量子态上直接进行加噪与去噪具有物理一致性，但应用于经典高维数据时受限于编码所需的量子比特成本以及模拟大密度算子的计算负担。

Method: 提出混合生成流水线：经典自编码器将高维数据压缩为紧凑潜在编码，嵌入到少量量子比特的希尔伯特空间中；混合态量子去噪扩散概率模型（MSQuDDPM）在潜在空间学习密度算子的生成分布；通过预测干净状态ρ₀并利用解析反向传播规则计算一步反向更新，替代显式学习ρ_{t-1}的预测器。

Result: 在MNIST图像生成任务上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合态量子扩散可作为混合量子-经典生成建模的实用主干，在现实量子比特预算下具有可行性。

Abstract: Quantum diffusion models provide a physics-consistent route to generative learning by formulating noising and denoising directly on quantum states. However, applying such models to classical high-dimensional data is constrained by the qubit cost of state encoding and the computational burden of simulating large density operators. We propose a scalable hybrid generative pipeline that combines a classical autoencoder for dimensionality reduction with a mixed-state quantum denoising diffusion probabilistic model (MSQuDDPM) operating in the learned latent space. The autoencoder compresses data into compact latent codes that can be embedded into a small-qubit Hilbert space, after which the quantum diffusion model learns a generative distribution over latent density operators and decodes samples back to the original domain. Algorithmically, we simplify the reverse dynamics by predicting an estimate of the clean state $ρ_0$ at timestep $t$ and computing the one-step reverse update via an analytic backward propagation rule, rather than learning an explicit predictor for $ρ_{t-1}$. We demonstrate the proposed approach on MNIST image generation and discuss how mixed-state quantum diffusion can serve as a practical backbone for hybrid quantum--classical generative modeling under realistic qubit budgets.

</details>


### [111] [FedCVESA: Taking Away Training Data in Federated Learning via Correlation Value Encoding and Segmented Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07314)
*Chongkai Li,Bang Zhang,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCVESA，一种针对联邦学习的白盒训练数据窃取攻击。恶意服务器通过皮尔逊相关正则化将目标客户端的私有训练数据编码进全局模型的选定载体参数中，并采用分段聚合策略防止参数被覆写。实验表明该方法能有效窃取语义级私有图像，同时保持主任务可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式TATD攻击已证明深度模型可被恶意训练用来记忆并恢复训练数据，但该记忆化威胁在联邦学习环境下尚未被系统研究，因为多客户端平均聚合可能覆写编码的训练数据。

Method: 提出FedCVESA：(1) 恶意服务器从K个客户端中选择n个目标客户端；(2) 在目标客户端的损失函数中加入皮尔逊相关正则化项，将私有训练数据逐步编码到选定的模型参数（载体参数）中；(3) 提出分段聚合策略，对分散的载体参数进行保护性聚合，其余参数保持标准平均聚合。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10数据集上的Dirichlet非独立同分布划分实验表明，该方法能在保持可接受主任务效用的前提下，从训练好的模型中窃取具有语义意义的私有训练图像。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在研究的白盒恶意服务器设定下，可成为参数级记忆通道，支持主动TATD攻击。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) avoids explicit data exposure by keeping raw data on local clients, yet privacy risks remain in the training process and the learned model itself. Recently, centralized Taking Away Training Data (TATD) attacks have shown that malicious training could abuse the memorization capacity of deep models to store and later recover training data. However, this memorization-based threat has not been systematically studied under FL environments, where multi-client averaging could overwrite encoded training data. In this paper, we study a white-box TATD attack in which a malicious server selects n target clients from K participating clients and actively writes private training data into the global model during federated training. We propose FedCVESA, a federated variant of Correlation Value Encoding Attack (CVEA), by adding a Pearson-correlation regularizer to the loss function of target clients, so that private training data are gradually encoded into selected model parameters, referred to as carrier parameters. To reduce the overwriting of carrier parameters during server aggregation, we further propose segmented aggregation over dispersed carrier parameters, preserving selected carrier parameters while keeping standard averaging on the remaining parameters. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 under Dirichlet non-IID partitions show that the proposed method can steal semantically meaningful private training images from the trained model while maintaining acceptable main-task utility in a controlled proof-of-concept setting. These results demonstrate that FL can become a parameter-level memorization channel for active TATD attack under the studied white-box malicious-server setting.

</details>


### [112] [Hypergraph Neural Stochastic Diffusion: An SDE Framework for Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07330)
*Zhiheng Zhou,Mengyao Zhou,Dengyi Zhao,Xingqin Qi,Guiying Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperNSD，一种基于随机微分方程的框架，用于超图上的不确定性估计，通过将超图表示建模为随机过程来量化预测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图神经网络虽能建模高阶关系，但其预测不确定性未得到充分探索。现有方法主要从最终预测中估计不确定性，或依赖计算昂贵的集成和贝叶斯推断，难以捕捉表示学习过程中的不确定性演化。

Method: 将超图表示建模为在节点-超边关联结构上演化的随机过程，学习漂移函数捕捉确定性高阶扩散动力学，学习随机强迫函数刻画结构模糊性和表示噪声，通过表示轨迹的变异性直接量化预测不确定性。

Result: 在多个超图基准上，HyperNSD实现了可靠的分布外和误分类检测的不确定性估计，同时保持了有竞争力的预测精度。

Conclusion: 提供了一个原则性的随机动力学框架，用于可信的高阶表示学习。

Abstract: Hypergraph neural networks have shown powerful capability in modeling higher-order relations, yet their predictive uncertainty remains underexplored. Unlike pairwise graphs, uncertainty in hypergraphs arises not only from noisy attributes and ambiguous labels, but also from variations in node-hyperedge incidence structures and complex higher-order dependencies. Existing approaches mainly estimate uncertainty from final predictions or rely on computationally expensive ensembles and Bayesian inference, limiting their ability to capture uncertainty evolution during representation learning. In this paper, we propose Hypergraph Neural Stochastic Diffusion(HyperNSD), a stochastic differential equation framework for uncertainty estimation on hypergraphs. HyperNSD models hypergraph representations as stochastic processes evolving over node-hyperedge incidence structures. A learnable drift function captures deterministic higher-order diffusion dynamics, while a learnable stochastic forcing function characterizes structural ambiguity and representation noise. Predictive uncertainty is directly quantified through the variability of stochastic representation trajectories, providing an intrinsic uncertainty measure beyond post-hoc confidence scores. We formulate HyperNSD with neural drift and diffusion networks, enabling joint learning of prediction and uncertainty propagation. Theoretical analyses establish well posedness, perturbation stability,permutation equivariance, and numerical convergence of the proposed stochastic dynamics. Experiments on multiple hypergraph benchmarks demonstrate that HyperNSD achieves reliable uncertainty estimation for out-of-distribution and misclassification detection while preserving competitive prediction accuracy. These results provide a principled stochastic-dynamical framework for trustworthy higher-order representation learning.

</details>


### [113] [Latency-Aware Bid Acceptance under Operational Feasibility: A Public Benchmark with Hindsight Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07343)
*Aswin Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FreightBidBench，一个基于公开数据、无需依赖私有数据的闭环卡车货运投标在线接受基准测试平台，并开发了两种事后上界及一种参数化替代滚动策略级联方法，在紧约束和稀缺运力场景下均能逼近滚动策略的收益，同时大幅降低决策延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卡车货运投标在线接受问题缺乏公开、可复现的基准测试：已有的路径规划基准是静态的，而动态车队研究通常依赖私有运营商数据，导致方法难以公平比较和复现。

Method: 1) 构建FreightBidBench基准：基于公开的货运分析框架（FAF）和美国农业部卡车费率数据，定义明确的可行性约束（取货可达性、时间窗、简化工时服务、随机堆场延误）和经济参数（服务失败罚金、终端车队价值、每日溢价窗）。2) 提出两种事后上界：简单LP松弛和更紧的拉格朗日每车信息松弛。3) 提出参数化替代滚动策略级联方法，包含边界带和稀缺压力升级触发机制。

Result: 拉格朗日信息松弛比LP松弛在紧运力场景下紧20.7%，在稀缺运力场景下紧39.3%。最优简单策略保留滚动策略收益的91.0%和86.5%；标准库替代策略保留94.2%和89.3%；单级级联策略在两种场景下均恢复约98%的收益，决策延迟仅为滚动策略的40-56%，且在紧运力场景下与滚动教师策略在统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: FreightBidBench为卡车货运在线投标接受问题提供了首个公开、可复现的基准测试平台，所提出的级联方法能够在接近滚动策略收益的同时显著降低计算延迟，为后续研究提供了标准化评估基础。

Abstract: Online truckload bid acceptance is a closed-loop stochastic decision problem in which a carrier or broker must, in real time, accept or reject a tendered load subject to operational feasibility, fleet repositioning costs, and opportunity cost against future demand. Public, reproducible benchmarks for this problem are scarce: existing routing benchmarks are static, while dynamic-fleet studies typically rely on private operator data. We introduce FreightBidBench, a public-calibrated, dependency-free, closed-loop benchmark in which feasibility (pickup reach, appointment windows, simplified hours-of-service, stochastic yard delays) and economics (service-failure penalty, terminal fleet value, daily price-premium window) are explicit, versioned, and reproducible from public Freight Analysis Framework and U.S. Department of Agriculture truck rate data. We develop two full-horizon hindsight ceilings: a simple LP style relaxation and a tighter Lagrangian-per-truck information relaxation that retains per-truck hours-of-service and sequencing structure and is 20.7% tighter than the LP relaxation on a tight-capacity scenario and 39.3% tighter on a scarce-capacity scenario. We introduce a parametric surrogate-rollout cascade with boundary-band and scarcity-pressure escalation triggers. On ten-seed tight and scarce scenarios, the best simple policy retains 91.0% and 86.5% of rollout profit and the standard-library surrogate 94.2% and 89.3%; a cascade at a single escalation band recovers roughly 98% on both at 40-56% of rollout's mean decision latency, and on the tight scenario is statistically indistinguishable from the rollout teacher (paired-bootstrap 95% CI on the profit delta spans zero).

</details>


### [114] [Multi-Agent AI Control: Distributed Attacks Hamper Per-Instance Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07368)
*Oliver Makins,Orazio Angelini,Zohreh Shams,Mary Phuong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次实证研究多智能体AI控制问题，开发了FakeLab合成实验室代码库，发现"碎片化效应"：随着攻击智能体数量增加，单智能体监控捕获攻击者的概率降低；显式规划器会放大此效应并将攻击完成率提升最多7倍；强监控模型可将未检测攻击降低一个数量级以上但仍不完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制技术主要研究单智能体单轨迹场景，但实际部署中多个智能体共享基础设施运行，最严重的风险（如模型权重窃取、训练投毒）需要多个智能体协同完成。因此需要研究多智能体场景下的AI控制问题。

Method: 开发了FakeLab合成AI实验室代码库（包含9项服务、86个良性任务、4种攻击目标），设计分布式攻击形式化框架，通过改变智能体数量、协调方式、模型能力和监控配置，系统性评估单智能体监控对抗分布式攻击的效果。

Result: 核心发现为"碎片化效应"：攻击智能体越多，每个智能体被监控发现的概率越低。显式规划器可将攻击完成率提升最多7倍，并加剧执行者较差的监控心智理论问题。弱"可信"监控器会漏掉大部分攻击，强监控器可将未检测成功降低一个数量级以上但仍不完美。

Conclusion: 多智能体场景下的AI控制面临新的挑战（碎片化效应），现有监控方法效果有限，需要进一步研究更有效的多智能体安全监控方案。

Abstract: AI control is a family of techniques to prevent an AI with malicious goals from subverting its operator's intent. AI Control usually studies a single agent in one trajectory, but real deployments run many agents over shared infrastructure, and the most severe risks (model-weight exfiltration, training-run poisoning) plausibly need several agents acting in concert. We initiate the empirical study of multi-agent AI control, formalising distributed attacks in which several agents jointly aim for a malicious goal. We develop FakeLab: a synthetic AI-lab codebase (9 services, 86 benign tasks, 4 attack objectives). We evaluate single agent monitoring against distributed attacks, varying the number of agents, their coordination, model capabilities and precise monitoring configuration.
  Our central finding is the fragmentation effect: as more agents coordinate to attack, per-agent monitoring becomes less likely to catch any of the attackers. Secondly, this is not driven by the ratio of benign to malicious code -- we argue this is likely dependent on model capability. Third, an explicit planner amplifies the fragmentation effect, raises the rate of completing the attack up to sevenfold, and mildly sharpens executors' poor monitor-theory-of-mind. Finally: a weak "trusted" monitor misses most attacks, while a stronger one cuts undetected success by more than an order of magnitude but remains imperfect. We are happy to share FakeLab, our control setting, with safety researchers on request.

</details>


### [115] [Fractal KV-Cache Archives: Lossless Symbolic Storage with In-Place Retrieval for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07144)
*Vladimir Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于迭代映射码的KV缓存存档格式，支持无损、线性时间、O(1)随机访问和O(1)均摊追加，结合每头残差向量量化在GPT-2上实现36-54倍压缩（困惑度损失11-15%），并发现键的量化损害约为值的4倍，同时该存档可作为子串搜索索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归推理中KV缓存占据主要内存成本，现有工作多聚焦于量化、驱逐或卸载等压缩方法，但忽略了量化后的符号流该如何存储的问题，且存储层是否可以承担更多功能。

Method: 采用收缩迭代映射码（contractive iterated-map codes）将符号序列序列化为低维实向量序列，形成KV缓存存档格式；结合每头残差向量量化（per-head residual vector quantization），保留少量精确窗口（4个attention sinks + 32个最近token），其余部分压缩存档。

Result: 在GPT-2 1024-token上下文上，存档KV缓存相比fp16基线压缩36-54倍，困惑度损失11-15%；量化键比量化值的损害约高4倍；存档同时支持近似子串查询，可直接在存储向量上执行搜索并解码匹配上下文。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅是一种高效的KV缓存存档格式，还具备搜索索引能力，且所有实验可在笔记本电脑CPU上单命令复现。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates the memory cost of long-context autoregressive inference, and a growing body of work compresses it through quantization, eviction, or offloading. We study a complementary question: once a position's KV state has been quantized to codebook indices, how should the resulting symbol stream be stored, and can the storage layer do more than store? A family of contractive iterated-map codes that serialize a symbol sequence into a sequence of low-dimensional real vectors is revisited, and it is shown that they form a natural archive format for a quantized KV cache with the following features. The method provides exactly the access pattern a growing cache requires. It is lossless, it runs in linear time, and supports O(1) random access and O(1) amortized append. A controlled study of the quantizer feeding this archive is conducted on GPT-2 with 1024-token contexts. Keeping a small exact window (4 attention sinks + 32 recent tokens) and archiving the rest, per-head residual vector quantization reduces the archived cache by 36-54x relative to an fp16 cache at a perplexity cost of 11-15%, and we quantify a sharp key/value asymmetry -- quantizing keys is roughly 4x more damaging than quantizing values, consistent with prior low-bit KV work -- and use it to allocate bits in a hybrid scheme. Finally, we show the archive is simultaneously a search index: approximate substring queries execute directly on the stored vectors, and matched context is decoded from the matched vector without ever materializing the surrounding text. We release all code; every number reproduces from a single command on a laptop CPU.

</details>


### [116] [Prior-matched evaluation of operational Earth-observation classifiers: a three-number reporting method demonstrated on Sentinel-1 internal-wave detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07146)
*Joao Pinelo,Joao Goncalves,Arun Shukla,Adriana Santos-Ferreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对地球观测中稀有事件检测的分类器评估问题，指出在类别不平衡条件下（正样本仅占5%），传统的平衡测试精度会严重高估模型的实际表现（0.794 vs 0.192）。作者提出一种"先验匹配报告"方法，通过三个指标（平衡测试精度、操作先验精度、真实部署精度）诚实评估模型，并在保持召回率≥0.80的条件下，将操作先验精度提升至0.927。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哨兵一号波浪模式存档中内孤立波检测任务面临严重的类别不平衡问题（正样本率仅约1/20），而分类器最初在1:1平衡数据上训练和报告，导致平衡测试精度严重高估实际部署性能。研究者需要解决这一评估偏差，建立更诚实的性能报告方法。

Method: 提出先验匹配报告方法，展示三个关键指标：平衡测试精度、操作先验精度和真实部署精度；在保持召回率0.80下限的前提下，实施精确度优先、泄漏控制的发展周期；每个改进环节仅针对预注册的边际增益进行验证；最后使用密封单读锁定箱进行认证。

Result: 在操作先验条件下精确度达到0.927；时间外检验确认判别能力可迁移到未见过的时间段，但固定操作点不能迁移；特征聚合是唯一带来实质性提升的改进手段，增加容量无效，校准无效。

Conclusion: 先验匹配报告方法——从平衡先验开始，随着数据流揭示真实分布逐步过渡到操作先验——可推广到任何在未知先验条件下启动稀有事件检测器的地球观测服务中。

Abstract: The Internal Waves Service screens the Sentinel-1 Wave-mode archive for internal solitary waves, routing detections to experts whose adjudication time is the resource the effort exists to conserve. Because attention is the cost of error, precision leads. Its classifier was trained and reported at a one-to-one class balance, fixed before the operational rate could be known. That rate has since emerged at roughly one scene in twenty, and a balanced-test score badly overstates the precision a validator meets. A model that scores 0.794 balanced-test precision scores 0.192 in real operation: the gap is a systematic artefact of reporting at the wrong prior, invisible to the metric most work quotes. We show the mismatch to be an evaluation problem in the costume of a training one at a fixed recall, prior correction and calibration cannot move precision, and answer it with a prior-matched reporting method based on three figures: balanced-test, operational-prior, and real post-deployment, whose contrast is the honest measure. A precision-first, leakage-controlled development cycle then improves the classifier lever by lever, each promoted only against a pre-registered margin; added capacity not clearing it, calibration inert, feature aggregation the one real lift, so the honest negatives are as much a result as the gain. Holding recall at a floor of 0.80 and certifying against a sealed, single-read lockbox, the promoted model reports 0.927 precision at the operational prior; an out-of-time check confirms discrimination transfers to unseen periods while a fixed operating point does not. Prior-matched reporting, begin balanced, then move to the prior as the stream reveals it, transfers to any operational Earth-observation service bootstrapping a rare-event detector under a prior it has yet to discover.

</details>


### [117] [RLVP: Penalize the Path, Reward the Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07435)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"惩罚路径，奖励结果"的方法，通过在强化学习中引入可验证的路径惩罚，解决了真实世界智能体部署中需遵守过程约束（如不重复呼叫、遵守营业时间等）且需从少量样本高效学习的问题，相比仅基于结果的RLVR方法，实现了高任务成功率与近零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界智能体（如自动拨打电话）面临两个关键挑战：(1) 部署可行性取决于过程路径而不仅是最终结果，存在大量与结果无关的约束（如不反复呼叫未应答用户、遵守营业时间、完成身份验证），仅基于结果的奖励无法表达这些约束，因为违反它们反而会提升表面成功率；(2) 每次交互成本高昂，智能体必须从极少的样本中高效学习。标准RLVR方法对这两个挑战均不敏感。

Method: 提出"惩罚路径，奖励结果"的配方：基于可验证的路径惩罚（过程约束）与结果奖励的联合优化。提供了四条有效惩罚的设计规则，包括避免单独使用惩罚时出现的"不作为陷阱"。该方法利用组相对优势等价于组内方差的原理，论证了稠密信号仅在能为结果缺失的方差提供补充时有效，而可验证的路径惩罚可靠地满足这一条件。

Result: 该方法实现了高任务成功率与近零违规，而仅基于结果的训练几乎在每个回合都违反约束。

Conclusion: 真实世界智能体部署中，应结合可验证的路径惩罚与结果奖励，而非仅依赖结果优化。四条设计规则为实际应用提供了系统指导。

Abstract: Agents acting on our behalf in the real world (e.g. placing phone calls) must learn online from costly, often irreversible interactions rather than cheap simulator steps. Two things follow. First, deployability depends on the path, not only the outcome. An agent must respect outcome-neutral constraints such as not repeatedly calling an unresponsive user, respecting business hours, or completing required authentication constraints that outcome-based rewards cannot express, since violating them frequently improves apparent success. Second, because each interaction is expensive, the agent must learn efficiently from very few examples.
  Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is blind to both challenges: it optimizes solely on the outcome and wastes expensive rollouts on all-fail groups where group-relative advantage collapses to zero. Attempts to densify supervision by rewarding progress target the hard-to-verify direction. In contrast, real agentic environments can cheaply detect bad moves. Since group-relative advantage is equivalent to within-group variance, a dense signal helps only when it supplies variance the outcome lacks. A verifiable penalty on the path meets this condition reliably, while a progress potential helps only where partial progress is reachable. The resulting recipe "penalize the path, reward the outcome" achieves high task success with near-zero violations, where outcome-only training violates constraints on nearly every episode. We provide four design rules for effective penalties, including avoidance of the inaction trap that arises when a penalty is used in isolation.

</details>


### [118] [Where to Intervene? Benchmarking Fairness-Aware Learning on Differentially Private Synthetic Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07471)
*Vinícius Gabriel Angelozzi,Héber H. Arcolezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了差分隐私合成数据上的公平性干预措施，发现后处理方法能在隐私-公平-效用之间取得较稳定的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）与公平性目标可能冲突：DP会放大不同人口群体间的差异，而现有公平性干预在DP约束下是否仍然有效尚不清楚。

Method: 以状态-of-the-art的边际DP合成器AIM为核心，在4个数据集上评估预处理、处理中、后处理三类公平性缓解策略，对比基线、仅DP、仅公平、DP+公平四种管线配置。

Result: DP单独会降低效用和公平性；公平性干预可部分恢复公平结果；后处理方法在不同隐私预算下提供更稳定的公平-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 后处理公平性方法在DP合成数据场景下效果更稳健，为隐私-公平-效用三难问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, raising concerns about both privacy and fairness. Differential Privacy (DP) has become a gold standard for privacy-preserving data analysis, while fairness-aware mechanisms aim to mitigate discrimination against underrepresented groups. However, these objectives can conflict: DP often amplifies disparities across demographic groups, and little is known about whether established fairness interventions remain effective under DP constraints. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic evaluation of fairness interventions on differentially private synthetic tabular data. Our benchmark centers on the Adaptive Iterative Mechanism (AIM), identified as the state-of-the-art marginal-based DP synthesizer (Cormode et al. 2025). We thus evaluate fairness interventions across four datasets, multiple group fairness metrics, and three categories of mitigation strategies (pre-processing, in-processing, and post-processing) under a wide range of privacy budgets. We compare four pipeline configurations: (Baseline) training on original data; (DP-only) training on DP synthetic data; (Fair-only) applying fairness mechanisms on original data; and (DP+Fair) combining fairness mechanisms with DP synthetic data. Our results demonstrate that while DP alone can degrade both utility and fairness, applying fairness interventions can partially restore equitable outcomes. Among them, post-processing methods tend to provide more stable fairness-utility trade-offs across privacy budgets and synthesizers, achieving strong fairness improvements while preserving competitive utility relative to other intervention stages. We release all code, data, and experimental artifacts in an open-source repository to ensure full reproducibility and to support future research on the privacy-fairness-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [119] [Geometric--Nongeometric Optimizer Calculus: A Modular Language for Reachable Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07206)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何-非几何优化器演算，一种用于审计可达梯度方法的模块化语言。该演算将优化器分解为几何模块（正协度度量族）和非几何模块（信息、记忆、控制、算子、噪声、目标和离散化机制）。主要形式化结果是方向表达性定理：远离临界点时，全正定几何精确表达了所有严格下降方向。论文定义了受限方向残差，证明了对角和块几何的精确表达性条件，并将方向级诊断与条件数几何复杂性分离。设计问题被重新定义为模块预算上的帕累托优化，而非单一通用优化器排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化器（如Adam、Muon等）混合了多种机制（度量、估计、记忆、步长控制、约束、随机性、目标修改和离散化），但缺乏一种统一的模块化语言来精确审计和理解这些优化器的可达方向空间及其约束条件。

Method: 引入几何-非几何优化器演算：几何模块（正协度度量族，将协向量映射到参数空间方向）与非几何模块（信息、记忆、控制、算子、噪声、目标和离散化机制）。通过方向表达性定理、受限方向残差、精确表达性条件等理论工具进行分析，并将残差从点级提升到轨迹级。

Result: 1) 全正定几何精确表达所有严格下降方向；2) 给出了对角和块几何的精确表达性条件；3) 将方向级诊断与条件数几何复杂性分离；4) 将设计问题定义为模块预算上的帕累托优化；5) 全度量探测器在确定性二次基准上达到数值精度；6) Muon风格的PyTorch候选在小规模上展示了矩阵算子更新可被审计。

Conclusion: 本文是一篇理论和基准语言论文，不声称大规模优化器的最优性能。其核心贡献在于提供了一种模块化演算语言，用于审计和理解不同优化器在约束下的方向表达性，并将优化器设计重新定义为多模块预算的帕累托优化问题。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers mix several mechanisms: a metric or preconditioner maps gradients to descent directions, while estimation, memory, step-size control, constraints, stochasticity, target modification, and discretization determine which directions are available and how they are used. We introduce geometric--nongeometric optimizer calculus, a modular language for auditing reachable gradient methods under explicit oracle, budget, state, and rule constraints. The geometric module is a positive cometric family that maps covectors to parameter-space directions; the nongeometric modules are information, memory, control, operator, noise, target, and discretization mechanisms. The main formal result is a direction-expressivity theorem: away from critical points, full positive-definite geometry expresses exactly the strict descent directions. We then define restricted direction residuals for admissible metric families, prove exact expressivity conditions for diagonal and block geometries, and separate this direction-level diagnostic from condition-number geometric complexity. The resulting design problem is a Pareto optimization over module budgets, not a single universal optimizer ordering. We also lift pointwise residuals to a trajectory-level residual complexity that couples direction mismatch with the variation of the explaining geometry. We include diagnostic prototypes only as evidence for the language: a high-information full-metric probe solves deterministic quadratic benchmarks to numerical precision, while a practical Muon-style PyTorch candidate gives small-scale evidence that matrix-operator updates can be audited by the calculus. The paper is a theory and benchmark-language manuscript; it does not claim large-scale optimizer state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [120] [TimEE: End-to-end Time Series Classification via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07500)
*Jaris Küken,Shi Bin Hoo,Martin Mráz,Frank Hutter,Lennart Purucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimEE是一个4.5M参数的基础模型，通过上下文学习实现端到端时间序列分类，仅在合成数据上元训练，在UCR基准上达到SOTA性能（ROC AUC第一，准确率第三）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分类采用两阶段范式（特征编码器+分类器），存在三个问题：表示学习与分类目标解耦、需要逐数据集训练、推理时无法利用标签信息。本文旨在通过上下文学习实现真正的端到端TSC。

Method: 采用Prior-Data Fitted Network (PFN)框架，仅在合成时间序列分类任务上进行元训练。每个合成任务包含由结构化分布偏移生成的不同类别的时间序列，模型只需一次前向传播即可输出查询样本的类别分布。

Result: 在UCR基准上，TimEE在所有对比方法（包括基础模型和有监督深度学习基线）中ROC AUC排名第一，准确率排名第三。这是首个纯合成预训练模型在UCR上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 端到端上下文学习结合合成先验是TSC的一个有前景的新方向，无需真实数据预训练即可达到SOTA。未来工作可沿扩展模型规模、改进先验设计、丰富生成机制等方向推进。

Abstract: Time series classification (TSC) is dominated by a two-stage paradigm: train a feature encoder -- either from scratch on the target dataset or via pretraining on large corpora -- and then fit a task-specific classifier on top. While effective, this decoupling optimizes representation learning independently of the classification objective, requires per-dataset training, and prevents the model from exploiting label information during inference. We introduce TimEE, a 4.5M-parameter foundation model for end-to-end TSC via in-context learning. Given a labeled support set and a query time series, TimEE directly outputs a predicted class distribution in a single forward pass with no per-dataset training required. Following the prior-data fitted network (PFN) framework, TimEE is meta-trained exclusively on synthetic TSC tasks, where each task contains time series with distinct class identities arising from structured distributional shifts in the generative process. Despite seeing no real time series during pre-training, TimEE ranks first in ROC AUC (and third on accuracy) on the UCR benchmark among all compared methods, which include both foundation models and supervised deep learning baselines. To our knowledge, TimEE is the first purely synthetic-pretrained model to reach state-of-the-art performance on the UCR benchmark. These results establish end-to-end ICL with synthetic priors as a compelling, largely unexplored direction for TSC, with scaling, prior design, and richer generation mechanisms as natural avenues for improvement. Code is publicly available at http://github.com/automl/timee.

</details>


### [121] [HPG-Diff: Hierarchical physics-guided diffusion with differentiable connectivity constraints for topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07233)
*Jinbo Yang,Mingyue Yuan,Boyuan Zhang,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Shikai Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出层次化物理引导扩散模型(HPG-Diff)，通过物理特征对齐和浮动材料抑制损失，在拓扑优化中实现0.87%(分布内)和5.29%(分布外)的平均合规误差，浮动材料比例降至2.90%和2.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型用于拓扑优化时缺乏物理引导，导致在未见过边界条件下泛化能力差，并产生浮动材料伪影。

Method: 提出HPG-Diff框架，包含两个协同机制：1)层次化物理引导策略，将预计算物理特征与去噪过程对齐；2)基于热传导的浮动材料抑制损失，从载荷位置模拟虚拟热传播过程，作为可微连通性约束惩罚浮动材料。

Result: 平均合规误差0.87%(分布内)和5.29%(分布外)；浮动材料比例降至2.90%和2.44%；通过LoRA微调可适应3:1矩形域(悬臂梁和桥基准)。

Conclusion: HPG-Diff通过物理引导显著提升了生成拓扑优化的泛化能力和拓扑连通性，轻量级LoRA微调可支持非方形域的适配。

Abstract: Deep generative models offer a promising paradigm for topology optimization, enabling rapid design exploration. However, these approaches lack intrinsic physics guidance, often leading to poor generalizability across unseen boundary conditions and the formation of floating material artifacts. To address these limitations, we propose Hierarchical Physics-Guided Diffusion (HPG-Diff), a novel diffusion framework that enforces physics consistency through two synergistic mechanisms. First, we introduce a hierarchical physics-guided strategy that aligns different precomputed physics features with the denoising process, guiding material distribution toward optimal load paths to enhance generalizability. Second, we propose a floating material suppression loss as a differentiable connectivity constraint inspired by thermal conduction to improve topological connectivity. By simulating a virtual heat propagation process from load positions, this mechanism explicitly penalizes floating material during training. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that HPG-Diff achieves average compliance errors of 0.87% (in-distribution) and 5.29% (out-of-distribution), while reducing floating material ratios to 2.90% and 2.44%, respectively. Furthermore, case studies on a 3:1 rectangular domain, including cantilever and bridge benchmarks, provide preliminary evidence that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning with a small dataset can support the adaptation of HPG-Diff to rectangular non-square domains.

</details>


### [122] [Single-Rollout Asynchronous Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07508)
*Zhenyu Hou,Yujiang Li,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单 rollout 异步优化（SAO）方法，通过单 rollout 采样和严格双面 token 级裁剪，解决了异步强化学习中的稳定性与 off-policy 问题，在 agentic 编码和推理基准上持续优于 GRPO 及其变体，并成功应用于 GLM-5.2 模型的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步 RL 系统注重吞吐量，但忽视了训练稳定性和任务有效性。GRPO 框架中的分组采样不适用于异步 agentic 训练，导致 off-policy 问题和稳定性挑战。

Method: 1) 用单 rollout 采样替代分组采样，每个 prompt 只用一个 rollout 以减少 off-policy 效应并提升泛化性；2) 引入价值模型训练设计来改进单 rollout 策略；3) 采用严格的双面 token 级裁剪策略来提升优化稳定性。

Result: SAO 能稳定训练一千步，在 SWE-Bench Verified、BeyondAIME、IMOAnswerBench 等 agentic 编码和推理基准上持续优于 GRPO 及其变体；成功部署于 GLM-5.2（750B-A40B）的 agentic RL 训练管线。

Conclusion: 单 rollout RL 在模拟在线学习和不断变化的环境中特别有效，SAO 为长时域 agentic 任务提供了一种稳定、高效的异步 RL 训练方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is becoming increasingly important for post-training large language models (LLMs). Previous RL pipelines for LLMs were mostly synchronous and batch-interleaved, which is inefficient for long-horizon agentic tasks. Recently, asynchronous RL has emerged as a more efficient alternative by updating the model as rollouts arrive. However, existing asynchronous RL systems often emphasize throughput, while leaving training stability and task effectiveness largely underexplored. For example, a key challenge is that group-wise sampling in the widely-used GRPO framework does not naturally fit asynchronous agentic training. In this paper, we present Single-rollout Asynchronous Optimization (SAO) to address the stability and off-policy challenges in asynchronous RL. To reduce off-policy effects and improve generalization, we replace group-wise sampling with single-rollout sampling, that is, using one rollout per prompt. We further improve this single-rollout strategy with practical value-model training designs. To improve optimization stability, we introduce a strict double-side token-level clipping strategy. SAO is able to train stably for one thousand steps and consistently outperform GRPO and its variants on agentic coding and reasoning benchmarks, such as SWE-Bench Verified, BeyondAIME, and IMOAnswerBench. We also demonstrate that single-rollout RL is particularly effective in a simulated online learning setting, where the model must adapt to changing evolving environments. To this end, SAO is successfully deployed in the agentic RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>


### [123] [Collaborative Synthetic Data Generation for Knowledge Transfer in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07565)
*Maximilian Andreas Hoefler,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedKT-CSD提出了一种基于神经图像压缩启发的一站式联邦学习框架，利用公共预训练自编码器作为共享潜在空间，通过传递类别条件潜在统计量并添加差分隐私噪声来生成合成数据集，在单轮通信中实现了低通信开销、对数据异构的鲁棒性和形式化隐私保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OSFL方法虽然通过合成数据集或蒸馏物聚合客户端知识，但大多缺乏形式化的隐私保证，无法同时实现低通信、对异构数据的鲁棒性和严格的隐私保护。

Method: 利用公共预训练自编码器作为共享潜在空间，客户端在单次前向传播中编码私有数据并计算类别条件潜在统计量，服务器通过安全聚合聚合统计量并添加校准的差分隐私噪声，再解码生成合成数据集用于训练全局模型。

Result: 在隐私约束下，FedKT-CSD在多种数据集和异构性设置中与无隐私保护的基线方法表现相当甚至更优，且能够扩展到大量客户端。

Conclusion: FedKT-CSD通过设计内置了形式化的(ε,δ)-差分隐私保证，同时保持了客户端计算和通信的轻量级，有效填补了一站式联邦学习中隐私保护的空白。

Abstract: One-shot federated learning (OSFL) addresses the communication overhead of federated learning by limiting training to a single round, but doing so without sacrificing model quality is non-trivial, particularly when client data distributions diverge. Recent work has addressed this challenge by aggregating client knowledge on the server through the construction of transferable synthetic datasets or distillates. However, most of these methods lack formal privacy guarantees, leaving a gap in jointly achieving low communication, robustness to heterogeneity, and rigorous privacy. We propose FedKT-CSD (Federated Knowledge Transfer via Collaborative Synthetic Data), a framework inspired by neural image compression that closes this gap by leveraging publicly pretrained autoencoders as a shared latent space. Each client encodes its private data in a single forward pass, computes class-conditional latent statistics, and transmits these to the server. The server aggregates these statistics via secure aggregation, adds calibrated differential privacy noise, and decodes a synthetic dataset for training a global model and further downstream tasks. This design provides formal $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy by construction, while keeping client-side computation and communication lightweight. Despite operating under privacy constraints, FedKT-CSD is competitive with and even outperforms non-private baselines across diverse datasets and heterogeneity settings, and scales to a large number of clients. Our code is available at: https://github.com/an7123/FedKT-CSD

</details>


### [124] [Safe Reinforcement Learning using Ideas from Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07252)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度强化学习与模型预测控制的通用框架，通过离线MPC计算定义可行状态-动作空间，并在训练和部署时使用安全滤波器将RL智能体的瞬时动作投影到该全局验证的可行集上，从而在保证严格安全约束的前提下实现强化学习的探索和策略收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在复杂信息物理系统和机器人领域具有重要应用价值，但其在主动学习阶段难以保证严格的硬安全约束，违反机械限制可能导致物理系统的不可逆损伤，因此亟需一种既能保持深度学习自适应高性能特性，又能提供形式化安全保证的通用框架。

Method: 利用系统动力学的数学模型，通过离线MPC计算定义全局可行的状态-动作空间（涵盖所有满足约束的状态与控制输入的安全组合）；在RL训练和部署过程中，通过安全滤波器将智能体的即时动作投影到该已验证的可行集上，确保探索严格限制在安全运行区域内。

Result: 在非线性1-DoF实验室测试平台上进行了系统性评估，实验结果表明该方法在物理硬件上成功实现了安全探索和稳定的策略收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用框架有效解决了强化学习在物理系统中探索时的安全约束问题，实现了DRL的自适应高性能与MPC的形式化安全保证的结合，并在实际硬件上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables the synthesis of control policies directly from data, making it highly appealing for complex cyber-physical systems (CPSs) and robotics. A persistent challenge, however, is ensuring strict, hard safety constraints during the active learning phase. In real-world physical systems, violating mechanical limits can cause irreversible damage, necessitating that exploration remains strictly within safe operational regions. We propose a generalized framework that combines the adaptive, high-performance nature of deep reinforcement learning (DRL) with the formal safety guarantees of model predictive control (MPC). Using a mathematical model of the system dynamics, offline MPC computations define a feasible state-action space, representing all safe combinations of system states and control inputs that guarantee constraint satisfaction. During training and deployment, the RL agent's instantaneous actions are projected onto this globally verified feasible set via a safety filter. We systematically evaluate our generalized approach on a non-linear 1-DoF laboratory testbed, demonstrating successful exploration and stable policy convergence on physical hardware.

</details>


### [125] [ALER-TI: Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07640)
*Xuan-Thong Truong,Trung-Kien Le,Tung Kieu,Thi-Thu Nguyen,Nhat-Hai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ALER-TI，一种检索增强的时间序列插补框架，通过对齐历史潜嵌入来补充局部上下文缺失，提升非平稳、弱时序相关场景下的插补性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列插补方法主要依赖受损输入序列内的局部时序上下文，但在实际场景中，时间序列常呈现非平稳动态、弱时序相关性和低频模式，仅靠邻近观测难以可靠重建缺失值。

Method: 提出ALER-TI框架，核心是潜嵌入对齐（LEA），通过在潜空间施加后验掩码，缓解受损查询与完整历史候选之间的表示不匹配；支持历史嵌入预计算和缓存以实现高效检索；模型无关，可通过轻量适配模块集成到多种插补骨干网络中。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上、不同缺失率下，ALER-TI一致地提升强基线模型性能，增强多种插补设置下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 检索增强策略能够有效弥补局部时序上下文的不足，为时间序列插补提供更可靠的缺失值重建，且具有良好的通用性和集成性。

Abstract: Deep learning has significantly advanced time series imputation, yet most existing architectures primarily rely on localized temporal context within the corrupted input sequence. This reliance can be limiting in real-world scenarios, where time series often exhibit non-stationary dynamics, weak temporal correlations, and infrequent patterns that are difficult to reconstruct from nearby observations alone. In this paper, we propose ALER-TI, Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation, a retrieval-augmented framework that explicitly leverages historical patterns to supplement degraded local context for more reliable missing-value reconstruction. The core of ALER-TI is Latent Embedding Alignment (LEA), which mitigates the representation mismatch between corrupted queries and complete historical candidates. By applying post-hoc masking in the latent space, LEA aligns candidates with the query's missingness pattern while allowing historical embeddings to be pre-computed and cached for efficient retrieval. ALER-TI is model-agnostic and can be integrated with various imputation backbones through a lightweight adaptation module. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets under different missing rates demonstrate that ALER-TI consistently improves strong baseline models and enhances robustness across diverse imputation settings.

</details>


### [126] [BubbleSH: A Dataset of Rising Bubbles with Deformable Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07275)
*Rachna Ramesh,Kiet Bennema ten Brinke,Douwe Orij,Ivo Roghair,Vlado Menkovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BubbleSH数据集，包含通过高保真直接数值模拟获得的瞬态三维气泡群动力学数据（轨迹、速度、形态），其中气泡形态使用球谐函数紧凑表示。该数据集适用于数据驱动建模，特别是生成模型对气泡群未来轨迹的预测，并提供了评估指标和基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡流中可变形气泡与周围液体强烈耦合，呈现复杂多尺度动力学，但缺乏兼具轻量化和物理表达力的高保真数据集来支持数据驱动建模，尤其是考虑气泡形态变形和相互作用的场景。

Method: 从高保真直接数值模拟（DNS）中获取周期性域内上升气泡群的瞬态三维数据，使用球谐函数紧凑表示气泡形态，提供时间分辨的轨迹、速度和形状演化信息，并引入置换和平移等变概率仿真器进行评估。

Result: 构建了BubbleSH数据集，对其进行了气泡运动学、形态和相互作用模式的特征分析，并提出了轨迹与形状预测的评估指标，验证了等变概率仿真器在该数据集上的有效性。

Conclusion: BubbleSH作为一个紧凑高保真的数据集和基准，为开发与评估可变形混沌多相系统的数据驱动模型（特别是生成模型）提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Bubbly flows exhibit complex multiscale dynamics, with deformable bubbles interacting through the surrounding liquid and giving rise to strongly coupled kinematic and morphological behavior. We present BubbleSH, a bubbly flows dataset consisting of transient, three-dimensional bubble-swarm dynamics obtained from high-fidelity direct numerical simulations of bubbles rising in a periodic domain. The dataset provides time-resolved bubble trajectories, velocities, and shape evolution, with bubble morphology compactly represented using spherical harmonics. Designed to be lightweight yet physically expressive, the dataset enables data-driven modeling of bubbly flow simulators where shape deformation and bubble-bubble interactions play a central role. We characterize the dataset with bubble kinematics, morphology, and interaction patterns, and introduce evaluation metrics for both trajectory and shape prediction. The sensitivity of bubble-swarm dynamics to local perturbations makes BubbleSH particularly well suited to generative models that learn distributions over possible future trajectories. We evaluate a permutationally and translationally equivariant probabilistic emulator on BubbleSH given the proposed metrics. Therefore, we establish a compact, high-fidelity dataset and a benchmark for developing and evaluating data-driven models of deformable, chaotic multiphase systems.

</details>


### [127] [Nonlinear Bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07304)
*Tianshuo Zheng,Ting Wu,Zhi-Hua Zhou,Keqin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对重尾噪声下的广义线性 bandit（GLB）问题，提出基于在线镜像下降（OMD）的 EHM 算法，利用自适应 Huber 损失实现单轮更新（O(1) 复杂度）并达到接近最优的遗憾界；进一步扩展至分段常数上下文场景（PGLB-EHM），及非线性 bandit 场景（NB-EHM），通过仿射提升方法实现次线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾分布在推荐系统、金融市场、医疗等现实场景中广泛存在，而现有广义线性 bandit 算法多假设噪声为轻尾（如次高斯），难以处理重尾噪声带来的稳定性与收敛性挑战。同时，许多算法需要预知噪声矩参数等先验信息，限制了实用性。

Method: 基于在线镜像下降（OMD）框架，结合自适应 Huber 损失方法，设计 EHM 算法实现单轮 O(1) 更新复杂度；利用特定链接函数的性质消除对常见参数的依赖。针对分段常数上下文场景，改进得到 PGLB-EHM；针对非线性 bandit，引入二分法与特殊约束得到 NB-EHM，并采用仿射提升方法将一般非线性 bandit 问题转化为可应用 NB-EHM 的形式。

Result: EHM 算法达到接近最优的遗憾界 Õ(T^{1/(1+ε)})；PGLB-EHM 保持相同的遗憾界上界阶数；NB-EHM 结合仿射提升方法实现次线性遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了重尾噪声下 GLB 及其扩展场景的在线学习问题，提出的算法在理论保证和计算效率上均具有优势，为实际重尾环境中的序贯决策提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In this paper we first study the problem of generalized linear bandit (GLB) under heavy-tailed noise. The characteristics of heavy-tailed distributions are widely observed in real-world applications such as personalized recommendation, financial markets, and medical treatments. Based on the online mirror descent (OMD) method, we propose an algorithm EHM that extends the adaptive Huber loss method (Wang et al., 2025) with one-pass update ($\mathcal{O}(1)$ computational complexity with respect to current round $t$ and the time horizon $T$), which simultaneously achieves an almost optimal regret of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{\frac{1}{1+ε}})$ where $T$ is the time horizon. In addition, by utilizing a special property of some link function (Sawarni et al., 2025), our algorithm eliminates the need to know a commonly used parameter. Next, we study the GLB problem under the case when contextual characteristic becomes piecewise constant, and we slightly revised former algorithm to obtain the PGLB-EHM algorithm. After theoretical analysis, we prove that the regret upper bound order stays the same. Furthermore, we look deeper into a special case of nonlinear bandit (NB) and present the NB-EHM algorithm with bisection method and special restriction. Eventually we utilize the affine lifting approach and show that the general NB problem can be applied with NB-EHM to achieve a sublinear regret bound.

</details>


### [128] [Selective Timestep Weighting and Advantage-Based Replay for Sample-Efficient Diffusion RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07693)
*Eric Zhu,Abhinav Shrivastava,Soumik Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补策略（逐时间步加权和基于信息量的轨迹重放）来显著提升扩散模型RLHF的反馈效率，在相同超参数下实现了最高6倍的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型RLHF方法反馈效率极低，需要大量人工或奖励模型评估，这限制了其在实际场景中的实用性，因为反馈是主要瓶颈。

Method: 1) 引入逐时间步加权方案，根据去噪步骤的信息量重新权重，并在理论上将其与PPO的最优收敛性质联系起来；2) 引入基于轨迹信息量的重放机制，优先复用信息丰富的轨迹，减少重复查询新奖励。

Result: 在相同超参数设置下，该方法相比广泛使用的扩散RLHF基线实现了最高6倍的样本效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过强调优化过程中信息丰富的去噪时间步和轨迹，可以获得更有效的梯度更新，从而在保持对未见提示泛化能力的同时大幅提升反馈效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for aligning generative models with human preferences. However, applying RLHF to diffusion models remains highly feedback inefficient, as existing approaches typically require large amounts of human or reward model evaluations. This limitation reduces the practicality of diffusion RLHF in realworld settings where feedback is the primary bottleneck. In this paper, we propose two complementary strategies that substantially improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF while preserving generalization to unseen prompts. Our key observation is that reward information in diffusion trajectories is unevenly distributed: not all denoising timesteps or trajectories contribute equally to learning from a reward signal. By emphasizing informative timesteps and trajectories during optimization, we obtain more effective gradient updates. First, we introduce a per-timestep weighting scheme that reweights denoising steps during policy optimization. We theoretically connect this weighting to the optimal convergence properties of proximal policy optimization (PPO) and approximate the resulting weighting trend empirically. Second, we introduce a replay mechanism that prioritizes informative trajectories, enabling the model to reuse past samples instead of repeatedly querying new rewards. Together, these strategies significantly improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF. Under identical hyperparameter settings, our approach achieves up to a 6$\times$ improvement in sample efficiency compared to widely used diffusion RLHF baselines.

</details>


### [129] [Mechanistic Interpretability for Neural Networks: Circuits, Sparse Features and Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07316)
*Pranav Sawant,Jakub Krejčí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了机械可解释性这一新兴领域，详细介绍了Transformer电路分析、叠加与多义性问题的解决方案（如稀疏自编码器）、模型行为控制方法（如因果干预与转向向量），以及将神经表示转化为显式逻辑规则的神经符号AI框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释AI方法仅停留在输入-输出相关性层面，无法揭示模型内部运作机制。在高风险部署场景中，确保机器学习模型的安全性和可审计性至关重要，因此需要一种能够逆向工程神经网络内部算法的系统性方法。

Method: 论文从三个层面展开：（1）Transformer电路分析——研究残差流、注意力机制和归纳头等内部组件如何驱动复杂任务和上下文学习；（2）叠加与多义性分解——利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和Transcoders将纠缠的神经激活分解为可解释的独立特征；（3）模型行为控制——通过转向向量和因果干预主动修改模型行为；最后与神经符号AI框架结合，将神经表示转化为显式的可执行逻辑规则。

Result: 论文系统性地梳理了机械可解释性的核心工具与方法论，展示了从微观电路分析到宏观行为控制的完整技术栈，并建立了与神经符号AI的桥梁。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性通过逆向工程神经网络内部算法，能够超越传统方法揭示模型的真实运作机制，为AI安全、审计和可控性提供了关键的技术路径。

Abstract: This article offers a comprehensive overview of mechanistic interpretability, an emerging field that seeks to reverse-engineer the internal algorithms of modern neural networks. While traditional explainable AI methods often stop at surface-level input-output correlations, this approach directly addresses the opaque "black box" nature of machine learning models, which is essential for ensuring safety and auditability in high-stakes deployments. The paper provides a detailed examination of Transformer circuit analysis, exploring how internal components like the residual stream, attention mechanisms, and induction heads drive complex tasks and in-context learning. It subsequently tackles the core challenge of superposition and polysemanticity, demonstrating how tools like Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) and transcoders can decompose tangled network activations into distinct, human-interpretable features. Furthermore, the paper explores methods for actively controlling and modifying model behavior through steering vectors and causal interventions. Finally, it connects these mechanistic insights with neurosymbolic AI frameworks designed to translate neural representations into explicit, executable logical rules.

</details>


### [130] [Generalist Vision-Language Models for Fast Radio Burst detection: a zero-shot benchmark against a specialized detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07382)
*Raiff H. Santos,Amilcar R. Queiroz,Tharcisyo S. S. Duarte,K. E. L. de Farias,Rafael A. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了小型通用视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在零样本、仅提示词条件下检测快速射电暴（FRB）的能力，无需微调和标注数据。在2000个样本基准测试中，Gemma 4 2B模型准确率达93.65%，与专用检测器SwinYNet（92.90%）无显著差异，且在结构化RFI和纯噪声上的假阳性率显著更低。通过修改提示词即可实现三类分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用深度学习检测器在FRB自动检测中表现优异，但依赖大量任务特定训练数据且无法在不重训练的情况下重新定义。为此，研究人员希望探索能否使用小型开源通用视觉-语言模型在零样本条件下完成FRB检测，以克服专用模型的局限性。

Method: 从3000个模拟L波段动态光谱（含FRB、结构化RFI和噪声）中构建2000个样本的平衡二分类基准。在两个VLMs（Gemma 4 2B和4B）上逐样本与专用检测器SwinYNet比较，采用零样本、仅提示词方式，不进行微调且不使用标注示例，返回结构化决策和自然语言理由。

Result: 默认阈值下，Gemma 4 2B准确率93.65%，与SwinYNet（92.90%）无统计显著差异；VLMs在结构化RFI上假阳性率显著更低（6.4% vs 25.0%），在纯噪声上无假阳性。SwinYNet保持完美概率排序（ROC-AUC 1.0000 vs 0.9482）。修改提示词后，同一模型可配置为三类分类，在3000个光谱上准确率达86%且无假阳性FRB。

Conclusion: 小型通用VLMs在零样本FRB检测中达到了与专用检测器可比的准确率，尤其在控制假阳性方面表现更优。仅通过重写提示词即可重新配置任务，无需重新训练，展现了通用模型在专业天文检测任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration radio transients whose automated detection increasingly relies on highly specialized deep learning models. These detectors achieve exceptional performance, but they require large task-specific training datasets and cannot be redefined without retraining. In this work, we evaluate whether small, open-weight, locally run generalist Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can detect FRBs in dynamic spectra under a zero-shot, prompt-only regime, with no fine-tuning and no labeled examples, returning structured decisions with a natural-language justification. From a controlled set of 3000 simulated L-band dynamic spectra containing FRBs, structured Radio Frequency Interference (RFI), and noise, we draw a balanced binary benchmark of 2000 samples and compare two such VLMs (Gemma 4 2B and 4B), sample by sample, against the state-of-the-art specialized detector SwinYNet. At the default threshold, Gemma 4 2B reaches an accuracy of 93.65%, with no statistically significant difference from SwinYNet (92.90%), while showing a significantly lower false-positive rate on structured RFI (6.4% vs. 25.0%) and no false positives on pure noise. SwinYNet retains a perfect probabilistic ranking on this benchmark (ROC-AUC of 1.0000 vs. 0.9482), a ceiling that the zero-shot VLM approaches from general-purpose pretraining alone. Rewriting the prompt alone reconfigures the same models for three-class FRB/RFI/noise classification on the full set of 3000 spectra, where they reach up to 86% accuracy without a single false FRB.

</details>


### [131] [Sparse Delta Memory: Scaling the State of Linear RNNs through Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07386)
